+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    // Each thread accumulates k in increasing order, preserving the reference
+    // algorithm's summation order for a given output element.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty);
+
+        tileA[ty][tx] = (row < rows && aColumn < N)
+                            ? A[row * N + aColumn]
+                            : 0.0;
+        tileB[ty][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N) ? B[bRow * N + col] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            sum += tileA[ty][k] * tileB[k][tx];
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (row < rows && col < N) {
+        C[row * N + col] = sum;
     }
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
 }
 
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+

-        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
-            printResults = true;
-        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {

+    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiThreadLevel);
+

+    int worldSize = 1;

+    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
+    if (mpiThreadLevel < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
+        mpiAbort("MPI implementation does not provide MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED");

+
+    // Rank zero is authoritative for command-line settings.  This also keeps

+[[noreturn]] void mpiAbort(const char* message, const int errorCode = 1) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "matmul: %s\n", message);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, errorCode);

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
+                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        mpiAbort(message);

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    cudaCheck((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    // Each thread accumulates k in increasing order, preserving the reference
+    // algorithm's summation order for a given output element.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty);
+
+        tileA[ty][tx] = (row < rows && aColumn < N)
+                            ? A[row * N + aColumn]
+                            : 0.0;
+        tileB[ty][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N) ? B[bRow * N + col] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            sum += tileA[ty][k] * tileB[k][tx];
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (row < rows && col < N) {
+        C[row * N + col] = sum;
     }
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
 }
 
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+

-    bool validate = false;

    19	constexpr int TileSize = 32;
    20	
    21	struct Options {
    22	    size_t n = 512;
    23	    bool validate = false;
    24	    bool printResults = false;
    25	    bool help = false;

    28	
    29	// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  The expression is
    30	// intentionally unchanged from the reference implementation so that -r
    31	// remains comparable across implementations.
    32	constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
    33	                                    const size_t j) noexcept {
    34	    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
    35	           static_cast<double>(N * N);
    36	}
    37	
    38	void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    39	    // The initialization is independent for every element and is consequently
    40	    // a useful CPU-side OpenMP stage while the MPI ranks are being launched.
    41	#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    42	    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
    43	        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
    44	            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
    45	        }
    46	    }
    47	}
    48	
    49	Options parseArguments(const int argc, char** argv) {
    50	    Options options;
    51	
    52	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
    53	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
    54	            char* end = nullptr;
    55	            const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(argv[++i], &end, 10);
    56	            if (end == argv[i] || *end != '\0' || value == 0) {
    57	                options.valid = false;
    58	            } else {
    59	                options.n = static_cast<size_t>(value);
    60	            }
    61	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
    62	            options.validate = true;
    63	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
    64	            options.printResults = true;
    65	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
    66	            options.help = true;
    67	        } else {
    68	            options.valid = false;
    69	        }
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    return options;
    73	}
    74	
    75	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    76	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    77	    std::printf("Options:\n");
    78	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    79	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    80	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    81	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
    82	}

    84	[[noreturn]] void mpiAbort(const char* message, const int errorCode = 1) {
    85	    std::fprintf(stderr, "matmul: %s\n", message);
    86	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, errorCode);
    87	    std::abort();
    88	}
    89	
    90	void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
    91	               const char* file, const int line) {
    92	    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
    93	        char message[512];
    94	        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
    95	                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));
    96	        mpiAbort(message);
    97	    }
    98	}
    99	
   100	#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
   101	    cudaCheck((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
   102	
   103	__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
   104	                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
   105	                                     double* __restrict__ C,
   106	                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
   107	    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
   108	    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
   109	
   110	    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
   111	    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
   112	    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
   113	    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
   114	    double sum = 0.0;
   115	
   116	    // Each thread accumulates k in increasing order, preserving the reference
   117	    // algorithm's summation order for a given output element.
   118	    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
   119	        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
   120	        const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty);
   121	
   122	        tileA[ty][tx] = (row < rows && aColumn < N)
   123	                            ? A[row * N + aColumn]
   124	                            : 0.0;
   125	        tileB[ty][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N) ? B[bRow * N + col] : 0.0;
   126	        __syncthreads();
   127	
   128	        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
   129	        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
   130	            sum += tileA[ty][k] * tileB[k][tx];
   131	        }
   132	        __syncthreads();
   133	    }
   134	
   135	    if (row < rows && col < N) {
   136	        C[row * N + col] = sum;
   137	    }
   138	}
   139	
   140	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
   141	                    const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
   142	    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
   143	
   144	    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
   145	        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
   146	            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
   147	            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
   148	
   149	            double expected = 0.0;
   150	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   151	                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
   152	            }
   153	
   154	            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
   155	            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) /
   156	                                             (expected + 1e-10));
   157	
   158	            if (relError > 1e-6) {
   159	                std::printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, "
   160	                            "got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
   161	                            i, j, expected, actual, relError);
   162	                return false;
   163	            }
   164	        }
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    return true;
   168	}
   169	
   170	size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
   171	    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
   172	        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
   173	    }
   174	    return a * b;
   175	}
   176	
   177	void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
   178	    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
   179	    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
   180	    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
   181	    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   182	        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
   183	    }
   184	}
   185	
   186	bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
   187	                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
   188	                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
   189	    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   190	    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   191	
   192	    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
   193	    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
   194	    size_t displacement = 0;
   195	    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
   196	        const size_t rows = baseRows +
   197	                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
   198	        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
   199	            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   200	            return false;
   201	        }
   202	        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
   203	        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
   204	        displacement += rows;
   205	    }
   206	    return true;
   207	}
   208	
   209	}  // namespace
   210

   324	    MPI_Bcast(B.data(), static_cast<int>(N), matrixRowType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   325	    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? A.data() : nullptr, rowCounts.data(),
   326	                 rowDisplacements.data(), matrixRowType,
   327	                 localA.data(), rowCounts[rank], matrixRowType, 0,
   328	                 MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   329	
   330	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceB, B.data(),
   331	                               matrixElements * sizeof(double),
   332	                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   333	    if (localRows > 0) {
   334	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceA, localA.data(),
   335	                                   localElements * sizeof(double),
   336	                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   337	
   338	        const dim3 block(TileSize, TileSize);
   339	        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((N + TileSize - 1) / TileSize),
   340	                        static_cast<unsigned int>((localRows + TileSize - 1) /
   341	                                                  TileSize));
   342	        matrixMultiplyKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(
   343	            deviceA, deviceB, deviceC, localRows, N);
   344	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   345	
   346	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC.data(), deviceC,
   347	                                   localElements * sizeof(double),
   348	                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
   349	    }
   350	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
   351	
   352	    MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), rowCounts[rank], matrixRowType,
   353	                rank == 0 ? C.data() : nullptr, rowCounts.data(),
   354	                rowDisplacements.data(), matrixRowType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   355	    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   356	    double elapsed = 0.0;
   357	    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
   358	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   359	
   360	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
   361	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceB));
   362	    if (localRows > 0) {
   363	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceA));
   364	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceC));
   365	    }
   366	    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
   367	    MPI_Type_free(&matrixRowType);
   368	
   369	    if (rank == 0) {
   370	        const double milliseconds = elapsed * 1000.0;
   371	        const double seconds = std::max(elapsed, std::numeric_limits<double>::min());
   372	        const double gflops = (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) *
   373	                               static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N)) /
   374	                              seconds / 1e9;
   375	        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
   376	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   377	
   378	        if (printResults != 0) {
   379	            print_results(C, "MatrixC");
   380	        }
   381	
   382	        if (validate != 0) {
   383	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   384	            const bool validResult = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
   385	            if (validResult) {
   386	                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   387	            } else {
   388	                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   389	            }
   390	            MPI_Finalize();
   391	            return validResult ? 0 : 1;
   392	        }
   393	    }
   394	
   395	    MPI_Finalize();
   396	    return 0;
   397	}

+[[noreturn]] void mpiAbort(const char* message, const int errorCode = 1) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "matmul: %s\n", message);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, errorCode);
+    std::abort();
+}

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
+                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        mpiAbort(message);
+    }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    // Each thread accumulates k in increasing order, preserving the reference
+    // algorithm's summation order for a given output element.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty);
+
+        tileA[ty][tx] = (row < rows && aColumn < N)
+                            ? A[row * N + aColumn]
+                            : 0.0;
+        tileB[ty][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N) ? B[bRow * N + col] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            sum += tileA[ty][k] * tileB[k][tx];
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (row < rows && col < N) {
+        C[row * N + col] = sum;
     }
 }

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
 }
 
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+

+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    if (localRows > 0) {

+                              localElements * sizeof(double)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceC),
+                              localElements * sizeof(double)));
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceB),
+                          matrixElements * sizeof(double)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));

+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+[[noreturn]] void mpiAbort(const char* message, const int errorCode = 1) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "matmul: %s\n", message);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, errorCode);
+    std::abort();
+}

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
+                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        mpiAbort(message);
+    }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    cudaCheck((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    // Each thread accumulates k in increasing order, preserving the reference
+    // algorithm's summation order for a given output element.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty);
+
+        tileA[ty][tx] = (row < rows && aColumn < N)
+                            ? A[row * N + aColumn]
+                            : 0.0;
+        tileB[ty][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N) ? B[bRow * N + col] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            sum += tileA[ty][k] * tileB[k][tx];
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (row < rows && col < N) {
+        C[row * N + col] = sum;
     }
 }

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
 }
 
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+

+                              localElements * sizeof(double)));

codex
Correctness checks pass for one, two, four, and eight MPI ranks, including ranks with zero assigned rows. I’m making one final kernel-level optimization now: each CUDA thread will accumulate four output rows from a shared-memory tile, reducing block overhead and improving occupancy while keeping each element’s `k` summation order unchanged.

+               const char* file, const int line) {

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sums[TileSize / 8] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    // Each thread computes four rows of one output column.  This retains the
+    // same k order for every element while using 256 threads per block rather
+    // than 1024, which gives the SM more resident warps and better occupancy.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+            const size_t aRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize +
+                                static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            tileA[ty + output * 8][tx] = (aRow < rows && aColumn < N)
+                                             ? A[aRow * N + aColumn]
+                                             : 0.0;
+            tileB[ty + output * 8][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N)
+                                             ? B[bRow * N + col]
+                                             : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+                const size_t rowInTile = static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+                sums[output] += tileA[rowInTile][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+        const size_t row = rowBase + static_cast<size_t>(output * 8);
+        if (row < rows && col < N) {
+            C[row * N + col] = sums[output];
+        }
     }
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
+}
+
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
 }

+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  The expression is

+// remains comparable across implementations.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
+                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        mpiAbort(message);

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sums[TileSize / 8] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    // Each thread computes four rows of one output column.  This retains the
+    // same k order for every element while using 256 threads per block rather
+    // than 1024, which gives the SM more resident warps and better occupancy.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+            const size_t aRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize +
+                                static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            tileA[ty + output * 8][tx] = (aRow < rows && aColumn < N)
+                                             ? A[aRow * N + aColumn]
+                                             : 0.0;
+            tileB[ty + output * 8][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N)
+                                             ? B[bRow * N + col]
+                                             : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+                const size_t rowInTile = static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+                sums[output] += tileA[rowInTile][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+        const size_t row = rowBase + static_cast<size_t>(output * 8);
+        if (row < rows && col < N) {
+            C[row * N + col] = sums[output];
+        }
     }
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
+}
+
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
 }

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
+                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sums[TileSize / 8] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    // Each thread computes four rows of one output column.  This retains the
+    // same k order for every element while using 256 threads per block rather
+    // than 1024, which gives the SM more resident warps and better occupancy.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+            const size_t aRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize +
+                                static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            tileA[ty + output * 8][tx] = (aRow < rows && aColumn < N)
+                                             ? A[aRow * N + aColumn]
+                                             : 0.0;
+            tileB[ty + output * 8][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N)
+                                             ? B[bRow * N + col]
+                                             : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+                const size_t rowInTile = static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+                sums[output] += tileA[rowInTile][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+        const size_t row = rowBase + static_cast<size_t>(output * 8);
+        if (row < rows && col < N) {
+            C[row * N + col] = sums[output];
+        }
     }
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
+}
+
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
 }

+                rank == 0 ? C.data() : nullptr, rowCounts.data(),
+                rowDisplacements.data(), matrixRowType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsed = 0.0;

+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceB));
+    if (localRows > 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceA));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceC));
     }
-    
-    // Validation
-    if (validate) {
-        printf("Validating result...\n");
-        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
-        
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
+    MPI_Type_free(&matrixRowType);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const double milliseconds = elapsed * 1000.0;
+        const double seconds = std::max(elapsed, std::numeric_limits<double>::min());
+        const double gflops = (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                               static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N)) /
+                              seconds / 1e9;
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults != 0) {
+            print_results(C, "MatrixC");
+        }
+
+        if (validate != 0) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool validResult = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
+            if (validResult) {
+                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {

+            }

         }
     }
-

 }

+[[noreturn]] void mpiAbort(const char* message, const int errorCode = 1) {

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
+                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        mpiAbort(message);
+    }

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sums[TileSize / 8] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    // Each thread computes four rows of one output column.  This retains the
+    // same k order for every element while using 256 threads per block rather
+    // than 1024, which gives the SM more resident warps and better occupancy.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+            const size_t aRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize +
+                                static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            tileA[ty + output * 8][tx] = (aRow < rows && aColumn < N)
+                                             ? A[aRow * N + aColumn]
+                                             : 0.0;
+            tileB[ty + output * 8][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N)
+                                             ? B[bRow * N + col]
+                                             : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+                const size_t rowInTile = static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+                sums[output] += tileA[rowInTile][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+        const size_t row = rowBase + static_cast<size_t>(output * 8);
+        if (row < rows && col < N) {
+            C[row * N + col] = sums[output];
+        }
     }
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
+}
+
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
 }

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
+                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sums[TileSize / 8] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    // Each thread computes four rows of one output column.  This retains the
+    // same k order for every element while using 256 threads per block rather
+    // than 1024, which gives the SM more resident warps and better occupancy.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+            const size_t aRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize +
+                                static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            tileA[ty + output * 8][tx] = (aRow < rows && aColumn < N)
+                                             ? A[aRow * N + aColumn]
+                                             : 0.0;
+            tileB[ty + output * 8][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N)
+                                             ? B[bRow * N + col]
+                                             : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+                const size_t rowInTile = static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+                sums[output] += tileA[rowInTile][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+        const size_t row = rowBase + static_cast<size_t>(output * 8);
+        if (row < rows && col < N) {
+            C[row * N + col] = sums[output];
+        }
     }
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
+}
+
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
 }

codex
The first pass of the register-blocked kernel exposed a row-coverage mistake in the boundary mapping: the new 4-row scheme needs eight threads in the block’s y dimension, not four. I’ve isolated it from the failed validation and am correcting that launch geometry before rerunning the checks.
apply patch

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sums[TileSize / 8] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    // Each thread computes four rows of one output column.  This retains the
+    // same k order for every element while using 256 threads per block rather
+    // than 1024, which gives the SM more resident warps and better occupancy.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+            const size_t aRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize +
+                                static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            tileA[ty + output * 8][tx] = (aRow < rows && aColumn < N)
+                                             ? A[aRow * N + aColumn]
+                                             : 0.0;
+            tileB[ty + output * 8][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N)
+                                             ? B[bRow * N + col]
+                                             : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+                const size_t rowInTile = static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+                sums[output] += tileA[rowInTile][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+        const size_t row = rowBase + static_cast<size_t>(output * 8);
+        if (row < rows && col < N) {
+            C[row * N + col] = sums[output];
+        }
     }
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
+}
+
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
 }

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceB));

+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceC));
     }

-        printf("Validating result...\n");
-        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
-        
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
+    MPI_Type_free(&matrixRowType);

+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const double milliseconds = elapsed * 1000.0;
+        const double seconds = std::max(elapsed, std::numeric_limits<double>::min());
+        const double gflops = (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                               static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N)) /

+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,

+[[noreturn]] void mpiAbort(const char* message, const int errorCode = 1) {

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
+                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        mpiAbort(message);
+    }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    cudaCheck((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sums[TileSize / 8] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    // Each thread computes four rows of one output column.  This retains the
+    // same k order for every element while using 256 threads per block rather
+    // than 1024, which gives the SM more resident warps and better occupancy.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+            const size_t aRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize +
+                                static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            tileA[ty + output * 8][tx] = (aRow < rows && aColumn < N)
+                                             ? A[aRow * N + aColumn]
+                                             : 0.0;
+            tileB[ty + output * 8][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N)
+                                             ? B[bRow * N + col]
+                                             : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+                const size_t rowInTile = static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+                sums[output] += tileA[rowInTile][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+        const size_t row = rowBase + static_cast<size_t>(output * 8);
+        if (row < rows && col < N) {
+            C[row * N + col] = sums[output];
+        }
     }
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
+}
+
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
 }

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
+                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sums[TileSize / 8] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    // Each thread computes four rows of one output column.  This retains the
+    // same k order for every element while using 256 threads per block rather
+    // than 1024, which gives the SM more resident warps and better occupancy.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+            const size_t aRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize +
+                                static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            tileA[ty + output * 8][tx] = (aRow < rows && aColumn < N)
+                                             ? A[aRow * N + aColumn]
+                                             : 0.0;
+            tileB[ty + output * 8][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N)
+                                             ? B[bRow * N + col]
+                                             : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+                const size_t rowInTile = static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+                sums[output] += tileA[rowInTile][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+        const size_t row = rowBase + static_cast<size_t>(output * 8);
+        if (row < rows && col < N) {
+            C[row * N + col] = sums[output];
+        }
     }
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
+}
+
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
 }

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
+                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        mpiAbort(message);
+    }

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sums[TileSize / 8] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    // Each thread computes four rows of one output column.  This retains the
+    // same k order for every element while using 256 threads per block rather
+    // than 1024, which gives the SM more resident warps and better occupancy.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+            const size_t aRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize +
+                                static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            tileA[ty + output * 8][tx] = (aRow < rows && aColumn < N)
+                                             ? A[aRow * N + aColumn]
+                                             : 0.0;
+            tileB[ty + output * 8][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N)
+                                             ? B[bRow * N + col]
+                                             : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+                const size_t rowInTile = static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+                sums[output] += tileA[rowInTile][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+        const size_t row = rowBase + static_cast<size_t>(output * 8);
+        if (row < rows && col < N) {
+            C[row * N + col] = sums[output];
+        }
     }
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
+}
+
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
 }

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
+                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        mpiAbort(message);
+    }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sums[TileSize / 8] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    // Each thread computes four rows of one output column.  This retains the
+    // same k order for every element while using 256 threads per block rather
+    // than 1024, which gives the SM more resident warps and better occupancy.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+            const size_t aRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize +
+                                static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            tileA[ty + output * 8][tx] = (aRow < rows && aColumn < N)
+                                             ? A[aRow * N + aColumn]
+                                             : 0.0;
+            tileB[ty + output * 8][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N)
+                                             ? B[bRow * N + col]
+                                             : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+                const size_t rowInTile = static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+                sums[output] += tileA[rowInTile][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+        const size_t row = rowBase + static_cast<size_t>(output * 8);
+        if (row < rows && col < N) {
+            C[row * N + col] = sums[output];
+        }
     }
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);

+size_t checkedProduct(const size_t a, const size_t b) {
+    if (a != 0 && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / a) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimensions overflow the host address space");
+    }
+    return a * b;
+}
+
+void checkMpiSize(const size_t N) {
+    // A contiguous row datatype lets MPI transfer a row without limiting the
+    // number of matrix elements to INT_MAX.  MPI still uses an int for the
+    // number of rows in Scatterv/Gatherv, so keep that conversion explicit.
+    if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        mpiAbort("matrix dimension exceeds MPI's row-count limit");
+    }
+}
+
+bool createRowDistribution(const size_t N, const int worldSize,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowCounts,
+                           std::vector<int>& rowDisplacements) {
+    rowCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    rowDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t rows = baseRows +
+                            (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+        if (rows > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        rowCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        rowDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += rows;
+    }
+    return true;
 }

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "%s failed at %s:%d: %s",
+                      expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(status));

+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const size_t rows, const size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[TileSize][TileSize];
+    __shared__ double tileB[TileSize][TileSize];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize + ty;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TileSize + tx;
+    double sums[TileSize / 8] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    // Each thread computes four rows of one output column.  This retains the
+    // same k order for every element while using 256 threads per block rather
+    // than 1024, which gives the SM more resident warps and better occupancy.
+    for (size_t tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += TileSize) {
+        const size_t aColumn = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(tx);
+        for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+            const size_t aRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileSize +
+                                static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            const size_t bRow = tileStart + static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+            tileA[ty + output * 8][tx] = (aRow < rows && aColumn < N)
+                                             ? A[aRow * N + aColumn]
+                                             : 0.0;
+            tileB[ty + output * 8][tx] = (bRow < N && col < N)
+                                             ? B[bRow * N + col]
+                                             : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const size_t tileWidth = min(static_cast<size_t>(TileSize), N - tileStart);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < tileWidth; ++k) {
+            for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+                const size_t rowInTile = static_cast<size_t>(ty + output * 8);
+                sums[output] += tileA[rowInTile][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    for (int output = 0; output < TileSize / 8; ++output) {
+        const size_t row = rowBase + static_cast<size_t>(output * 8);
+        if (row < rows && col < N) {
+            C[row * N + col] = sums[output];
+        }
     }
 }
